- **vikunja-ticket**: Create a task in a tenant's Vikunja instance from any agent runtime that does not hold the vikunja_create_task tool natively — Claude Code and every OpenCode/Crush agent the server isn't wired into — through bin/mcp-call.py vikunja, the same vault-resolved launcher the native tool uses. - **vikunja-user**: Manage a user account's full lifecycle in a tenant's Vikunja instance via bin/vikunja-manage-user.py -- create, list, update, enable/disable, delete, reset-password, set-admin. Vikunja has no admin REST API for any of this (registration is disabled and its /register endpoint is disabled right along with it, by upstream design) -- the only path is the vikunja user CLI, run on the host over SSH + pct exec. Use for: vikunja user, add vikunja user, create vikunja account, vikunja invite, disable vikunja user, delete vikunja user, reset vikunja password, vikunja set-admin ([CascadeSTEAM/opskit](https://github.com/CascadeSTEAM/opskit/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/vikunja-user))
